THE DESIGN QUESTION (protocol, section 7, amendments 1 and 2).  L = ln(N_const/N_fluct) for one phase or schedule; a treatment's outcome
  is the mean of ln(count) over its replicates.  For two alternatives A and B and two treatments T1, T2: contrast = L(T2) - L(T1);
  Delta = contrast under A minus contrast under B.  Replicates per treatment for the two predictions to be 4 standard errors apart:
  n   measurement error only (sd of ln count 0.198).  Valid when the phases or schedules are balanced by design and recorded, and
      the prediction is made for them: a square wave with replicates spread evenly over its phases, or one drawn set of schedules shared by all.
  nm  replicates in mirrored pairs (a phase and the phase half a period later; a schedule and the same switching times with high and low
      exchanged), pairs drawn independently, their spread treated as error.
  n*  replicates with independent phases or schedules, not paired, their spread treated as error.
  ns  every replicate compared with the prediction for its own phase or schedule (uses Delta^2 + sdD^2); for (a) to (c) only: against
      'no effect' it would only show that growth follows the level of density.
  sdD = sd over phases or schedules of the difference between the two alternatives.
  'any' = any schedule of the grid (the protocol's search).  'adm' = admissible schedules only: square wave with at least two periods in
  60 days (period <= 30 d), telegraph with mean dwell <= 15 d.  A schedule that hardly switches within the run compares constant densities.
  'held' = densities held constant for the whole run at 1.8, 0.2, 1.5 or 0.5 times the mean (and the mean itself): not variability
  treatments; they measure how growth responds to the level of density.
  admissible for b = 2: square periods 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2, 4 gen; telegraph dwells 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2 gen
  admissible for b = 10: square periods 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2 gen; telegraph dwells 1/4, 1/2, 1 gen
  admissible for b = 50: square periods 1/4, 1/2 gen; telegraph dwells 1/4 gen
  (a) waste (A) against wait (B)
  (a) shapes (1,1), d=0.1  b=2  all parasites any  max Delta: day 60, [constant] vs [telegraph 1.8/0.2 dwell 8 gen]: contrast A +1.820, B +1.261, Delta 0.559, sdD 5.263, n = 5, ns = 1, nm = 5, n* = 2778
  (a) shapes (1,1), d=0.1  b=2  all parasites adm  max Delta: day 60, [constant] vs [telegraph 1.8/0.2 dwell 2 gen]: contrast A +1.587, B +1.078, Delta 0.509, sdD 3.196, n = 5, ns = 1, nm = 8, n* = 1231
  (a) shapes (1,1), d=0.1  b=2  all parasites adm  min nm   : day 60, [constant] vs [square 1.8/0.2 period 4 gen]: contrast A +1.533, B +1.028, Delta 0.505, sdD 0.392, n = 5, ns = 4, nm = 5, n* = 27
